The board is made of columns (statuses) and cards. Cards can be moved within a column and across columns. Ordering is stored as a fractional floating-point `position`, so moving a card only ever updates that single card\'s `position` and never renumbers its siblings.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'The app uses username & password authentication, a SQLite database (the Wasp development default), Prisma for the data model, and Wasp Operations for the API. All board data is private to the authenticated user who owns it.\n'[39m +

                [32m'## Requirements\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Data model (`schema.prisma`):\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * `Column`: `id` (Int, primary key), `title` (String), `position` (Float), and an owner relation to `User`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * `Card`: `id` (Int, primary key), `title` (String), `position` (Float), a relation to its `Column` exposed through a `columnId` (Int) foreign key, and an owner relation to `User`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * The `User` entity required by Wasp auth.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Enable username & password authentication. Every operation below must act only on data owned by the currently authenticated user.\n'[39m +

                [32m'  * `POST http://localhost:3001/operations/get-board`\n'[39m +

                [32m'  * `POST http://localhost:3001/operations/move-card`\n'[39m +

                [32m'* Any of the four operations invoked without a valid session must fail with `HttpError` status `401`.\n'[39m +
                [32m"* `getBoard` (no payload) returns the current user's board as a JSON array of column objects ordered by `position` ascending. Each column object must contain at least the keys `id`, `title`, `position`, and `cards`, where `cards` is an array of that column's cards ordered by `position` ascending. Each card object must contain at least the keys `id`, `title`, `position`, and `columnId`.\n"[39m +
                [32m'* `createColumn` (payload keys: `title` string, `position` number) creates a column owned by the current user at the given position and returns the created column object including `id`, `title`, `position`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* `createCard` (payload keys: `title` string, `columnId` number, `position` number) creates a card with the given title and position inside the column identified by `columnId`, and returns the created card object including `id`, `title`, `position`, `columnId`. If `columnId` does not identify a column owned by the current user, the call must fail with `HttpError` status `403` or `404`.\n'[39m +
                [32m"* `moveCard` (payload keys: `cardId` number, `targetColumnId` number, `afterCardId` number optional, `beforeCardId` number optional) moves the card identified by `cardId` into the column identified by `targetColumnId` and assigns it a new fractional `position` according to the rules below. It updates only the moved card: the card's `columnId` becomes `targetColumnId` and its `position` is recomputed. No other card's `position` may change. It returns the updated card object including `id`, `title`, `position`, `columnId`. `moveCard` does not accept a caller-supplied position; the server computes it.\n"[39m +
                [32m'  * `afterCardId`, when provided, identifies the card that must end up immediately before the moved card (i.e. with a strictly smaller `position`).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * `beforeCardId`, when provided, identifies the card that must end up immediately after the moved card (i.e. with a strictly larger `position`).\n'[39m +
                [32m"  * When both `afterCardId` and `beforeCardId` are provided, the moved card's new `position` must equal exactly the arithmetic mean of those two neighbors' `position` values.\n"[39m +
                [32m"  * When only `afterCardId` is provided, the moved card is placed at the tail of the target column: its new `position` must be strictly greater than that neighbor's `position`.\n"[39m +
                [32m"  * When only `beforeCardId` is provided, the moved card is placed at the head of the target column: its new `position` must be strictly smaller than that neighbor's `position`.\n"[39m +
                [32m'  * When neither neighbor is provided, the moved card is the only ordering reference in the target column and may take any valid `position`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * `cardId` and `targetColumnId` must identify a card and a column owned by the current user. `afterCardId` and `beforeCardId`, when provided, must identify cards that currently belong to the target column and are owned by the current user.             [32m'   - This is a safety feature to prevent unintended replacements\n'[39m +

            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'# Committing changes with git\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +

            [32m'- NEVER skip hooks (--no-verify, --no-gpg-sign, etc) unless the user explicitly requests it\n'[39m +

Instead, after hook failure, fix the issue, re-stage, and create a NEW commit\n'[39m +
            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +

If `columnId` does not identify a column owned by the current user, the call must fail with `HttpError` status `403` or `404`.\n'[39m +
                [32m"* `moveCard` (payload keys: `cardId` number, `targetColumnId` number, `afterCardId` number optional, `beforeCardId` number optional) moves the card identified by `cardId` into the column identified by `targetColumnId` and assigns it a new fractional `position` according to the rules below. It updates only the moved card: the card's `columnId` becomes `targetColumnId` and its `position` is recomputed. No other card's `position` may change. It returns the updated card object including `id`, `title`, `position`, `columnId`. `moveCard` does not accept a caller-supplied position; the server computes it.\n"[39m +
                [32m'  * `afterCardId`, when provided, identifies the card that must end up immediately before the moved card (i.e. with a strictly smaller `position`).\n'[39m +

                    [32m' *\n'[39m +
                    [32m' * A page is a React component rendered by a {@link route}.\n'[39m +
                    [32m' *\n'[39m +

            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'# Committing changes with git\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +

            [32m'- NEVER update the git config\n'[39m +

            [32m'- NEVER skip hooks (--no-verify, --no-gpg-sign, etc) unless the user explicitly requests it\n'[39m +

Instead, after hook failure, fix the issue, re-stage, and create a NEW commit\n'[39m +
            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +
